#upgrade palace
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoHaremVisit	
			value = 1
			}
		}
			}	
	picture = COURT_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	NOT = {

			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 21
			}
			}
		}

	}

			value = 41
			}
		}
		}	
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1D_eventPicture
	}

		}
		}

	NOT = {

			which = cosmoHaremVisit
			value = 141
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_MNG_CONCUBINES_VISIT_1B_eventPicture
	}

	hidden = yes

		ai = no
	}	


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_1	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_1_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_2	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_2_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_3	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_3_upgrade
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesUpgrade
		value = 1
		}		
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_4	
		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_4_upgrade
		}	
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_5

		value = 1
		}		
		}

		}	
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_all_upgrade
		}
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_1
				clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_2

		}

#Start event from estate
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.10
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
		has_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
	}
	has_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
			OR = {
				culture_group = central_american		
				culture_group = aridoamerican		
				culture_group = maya		
				culture_group = otomanguean
				culture_group = andean_group
				culture_group = je_tupi		
				culture_group = je	
				culture_group = maranon		
				culture_group = chibchan		
				culture_group = mataco			
				culture_group = araucanian		
				culture_group = carribean		
			}		
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 1
		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		set_ruler_flag = visiting_concubines
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_wives_launch
		remove_estate_privilege= estate_wives_ruler_visit
		if = {
			limit = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			}
			random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = ruler_palace_mil
	
				}																				
			}
		}		
		}
		}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.10.a	
		if = {
			limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.30
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_concubines_in_capital #china
				has_country_flag = cosmo_pirate_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_euroharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_eurowives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = comso_ooku_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_africawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_purconcubines_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_menaharem_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
				has_country_flag = cosmo_indiaharem_in_capital
			}	
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.10100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_isolation_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_advisors_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_onsenaffairs_events.401
			}
		}		
		if = {
			limit = {
		OR = {
			has_ruler_flag = ruler_renovate_concubines
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1
			}
		}																										
	}					
}

#grant wives 1a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.11
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.11.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.11.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_1A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chichimecan
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chichimecan
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chichimecan
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chichimecan
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chichimecan
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chichimecan
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_1a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.11.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chichimecan
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = chichimecan
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_1a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.11.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}						
		}					
}

#grant wives 1c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.13
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.13.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.13.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_1C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = tepic
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 20
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = tepic
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = tepic
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = tepic
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = tepic
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = tepic
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_1c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.13.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = tepic
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = tepic
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_1c								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.13.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

			OR = {
				culture = purepecha
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = purepecha
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = purepecha
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = purepecha
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = purepecha
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = purepecha
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

#grant wives 4a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.41
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.41.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.41.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_4A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_4a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = matlatzinca
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = matlatzinca
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = matlatzinca
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_4a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_4a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = matlatzinca
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = matlatzinca
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = matlatzinca
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_4a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_4a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}

#grant wives 5a
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.51
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.51.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.51.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_5A_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_5a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chontales
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chontales
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chontales
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_5a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_5a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chontales
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chontales
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chontales
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_5a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_5a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.51.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chontales
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = chontales
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_5a								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.51.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}

			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_6a
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = putun
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = putun
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = putun
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_6a
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_6a
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = putun
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = putun
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = putun
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_6a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_6a_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}

					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes

			}

		if = {

				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}

			add_province_modifier = {

			}
		}
		}
		if = {

			}	
			}

		}

		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_6a

#grant wives 7b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.72
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.72.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.72.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_7B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_7b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = miskito
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = miskito
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = miskito
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_7b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_7b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miskito
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miskito
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = miskito
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_7b
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_7b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}

#grant wives 8b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.82
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.82.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.82.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_8B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_8b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = guajiro
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = guajiro
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = guajiro
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_8b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_8b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guajiro
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guajiro
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guajiro
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_8b
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_8b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.82.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = guajiro
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = guajiro
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_8b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.82.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10

		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

#grant wives 9b
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.92
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.92.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.92.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_9B_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_9b
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chimuan
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = chimuan
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chimuan
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_9b
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_9b
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chimuan
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chimuan
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = chimuan
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_9b
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_9b_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.92.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = chimuan
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}	
		#set_ruler_flag = has_lowborn_consort
		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = chimuan
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_9b								
		country_event = { 
			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		}					
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.92.b
		tooltip = {
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1003
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1003.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1003.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_10C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_10c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mapuche
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = mapuche
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = mapuche
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_10c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_10c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = mapuche
			}

#grant wives 11c
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1013
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1013.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1013.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_11C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_11c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = guarani
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = guarani
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = guarani
				}
		}
			
	}

#		mean_time_to_happen = {
#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_11c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_11c
		}
		set_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_in_capital
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guarani
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guarani
				development = 9
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = guarani
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_forbiddenpalace
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_harem
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_ooku
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}
		add_estate_influence_modifier = {
			estate = estate_wives
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}		
			event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = cosmo_has_consort_present
				duration =  -1
				hidden = yes
				}				
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_11c
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_11c_DESC
				}			
			}																													
		}
	}	
	
	option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1013.a
		trigger = {
			NOT = {
				has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			}
			NOT = {
				any_subject_country = {
				OR = {
				primary_culture = guarani
				}
					cosmo_daimyo_personal_union_subject_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			event_target:consortprovince = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}
		}	
		event_target:consortprovince = {
			add_province_modifier = {
				name = "domain_of_spouses_family"
				duration = -1
			}
		}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {

			NOT = {
				owned_by = ROOT		
			}	
			}
		}

		}					
		define_consort = {
			culture = guarani
		}
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_consort
		set_consort_flag = cosmo_sawives_11c

country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1023
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1023.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1023.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_12C_1_eventPicture
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		OR = {
			has_estate = estate_forbiddenpalace 
			has_estate = estate_harem 
			has_estate = estate_ooku 
			has_estate = estate_wives 
			has_estate = estate_nobleaffairs
			has_estate = estate_harem 
		}
		NOT = {
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12c
		}
		}
		
		OR = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = maipurean
			}
			}
			any_subject_country = {
			development_in_provinces = {
				value = 6
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				culture = maipurean
			}
			}				
			}			
				OR = {
				primary_culture = maipurean
				}
		}
			
	}

#			days = 30
#		}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		every_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12c
			}
			remove_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12c

				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT

			limit = {

			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				culture = maipurean
				development = 15
			}
				save_event_target_as = consortprovince
		}

#loop to begining
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hidden = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_1_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1a
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1b
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_2_upgrade
				has_country_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_all_upgrade
			}
		any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			OR = {
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2a
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2b
				has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2c
			}
		}	
		}	
		set_ruler_flag = cosmo_sawives_menu_2
		}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesSixBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_1c
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.130.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoSACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesOneCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesOneCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_eurowese_diplomat
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesOneCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}	
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesOneCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_2a
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}		
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.210.a	   
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoSACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesTwoACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesTwoACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesTwoACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_11c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_12c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_13a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_13b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_13c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_14a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_14b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_14c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15c
					}
				}
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}

				}

		}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFourACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			}

			}

				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoSACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFiveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesFiveCCounter	
			value = 460
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFiveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_13c
					}
				}				
				any_province = {

					}
				}				
				any_province = {

					}
				}

				}					   
			}

		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesSevenBCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesSevenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_natural_scientist
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesSevenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesSevenBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesTwelveACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesTwelveACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}

		limit = {

			which = cosmoSAWivesThirteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesThirteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFourteenACounter
		value = 0
			}

	}		   		
	}

					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15b
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15b
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1520
			}
		}
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1500.c
		if = {
			limit = {
		NOT = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15c
		}
		}		
		}
			custom_tooltip = concubine_gone_tt 		
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
					any_province = {
			country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_15c
		}		
		}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1530
			}
		}
		}		
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1500.7
			if = {
				limit = {					
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16c
					}
				}
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1500
			}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
			NOT = {
				any_province = {
					country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
					OR = {
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16a
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16b
						has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16c
					}
				}				
			}																
				}
			country_event = {
			id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.101
			}
			}																								
		}

		}

			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}

		limit = {

			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesFifteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesSixteenACounter
			value = 281
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_16A_11_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	goto = consortprovince

	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = sa_concubine_16a
			}
					save_event_target_as = consortprovince		
		}																
		}
	}		
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.1610.a	   
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_visit_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = ruler_mandate_concubines	
			}
			concubine_visit_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoSACounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesSixteenACounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesSixteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_european_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoSAWivesSixteenACounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoSAWivesSixteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}	
}

#grant wives cheat
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.999
	title = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.999.t
	desc = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.999.d
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_SA_MENU_6B_1_eventPicture

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no			
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
																													
		}
	}	
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_sawivesestate_events.999.a
		capital_scope = {
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_1a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_1a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_1b
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_1b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_1c
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_1c_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_2a
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_2a_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = { 
				name = sa_concubine_2b
				duration =  -1
				desc = sa_concubine_2b_DESC
				}
			add_province_modifier = {

				duration =  -1

		}

			id = cosmo_samarriage_events.101
			}
		clr_ruler_flag = xibei_consort
		clr_ruler_flag = jin_consort

			estate = estate_madams
			desc = EST_VAL_NEW_CONUBINE_NA
			influence = -10
			duration = 3650
		}					
		}
